Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a clouds algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 261. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 103/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_261(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 261. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^103(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any clouds module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 208. The projective resolution has length at most 115 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 208 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the clouds structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 115. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
